To execute attention with the same lookup-table machinery as ternary projections, values accumulated in one reduction must retain a compatible representation and scale. This requirement also differs for keys and values during causal decoding, because newly generated values may belong to an unfinished cache block. This work develops a unified lookup-table inference approach for ternary LLMs. It stores runtime K/V states as scaled multi-plane signed digits organized around the reduction structure of attention. The resulting digit planes are consumed directly by activation-derived tables, avoiding dense K/V materialization between cache storage and attention computation. The design combines online K/V formation, bounded handling of incomplete value blocks, and a shared multi-stream datapath for Linear projections and attention. A constraint-guided search selects the representation and execution policy for a target quality--efficiency trade-off. Experiments on native and post-training ternary models validate the approach across cache capacity, model quality, and hardware efficiency.

</details>


### [245] [Fovea: Physical-Implication-Aware Wafer-Scale DSE with Decision-Domain-Guided Cross-Fidelity Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03285)
*Jinxi Li,Huizheng Wang,Jinyi Deng,Yang Hu,Shouyi Yin*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了Fovea方法，用于晶圆级系统的架构选择，通过物理约束感知的设计空间构建和跨保真度优化，在保持最优解的同时实现显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代硅前设计空间探索采用从粗到细的工作流程，但晶圆级系统对两个阶段都带来挑战：架构选择会引起物理实现的耦合约束，使得设计空间无法作为无约束的笛卡尔积处理；同时详细评估成本过高，而分析模型与参考评估之间的排序反转使得固定候选列表不可靠。

Method: Fovea包含两个核心步骤：(1)物理约束感知的设计空间构建，构建建模可行的空间并保留跨维度权衡，仅应用保留评估器的局部剪枝；(2)决策域引导的跨保真度优化，通过配对域内校准估计工作负载特定的分析-参考评估偏差，参数化参考一致的性能区间并诱导决策域，从而选择性进行参考评估。

Result: 在10个参考可验证设计空间和7个LLM训练工作负载上，Fovea使用10%配对校准样本，在所有70个评估对中均成功恢复穷举参考最优解，同时实现平均4.13倍、最高7.80倍的端到端加速。

Conclusion: Fovea作为可重用的方法学，能够有效处理晶圆级架构选择中的物理约束耦合和评估成本挑战，在保证找到最优解的前提下显著提升设计空间探索效率。

Abstract: Modern pre-silicon design-space exploration (DSE) follows a coarse-to-fine workflow: low-cost evaluators screen candidate spaces, while detailed evaluation is reserved for a shortlist. Wafer-scale systems strain both stages. Architecture choices induce coupled physical implications for reticle compliance, wafer tiling, die area, D2D capability, boundary access, and placement, so the design space cannot be treated as an unconstrained Cartesian product. Meanwhile, detailed evaluation is too expensive to cover the resulting space, whereas analytical-to-reference ranking inversions make a fixed shortlist unreliable. We present Fovea, a reusable methodology for workload-specific wafer architecture selection rather than a fixed wafer template. Fovea first performs physical-implication-aware design-space formulation to construct a distinct modeled-feasible space while preserving cross-dimensional trade-offs and applying only evaluator-preserving local reductions. It then performs Decision-Domain-guided cross-fidelity refinement. Paired in-domain calibration estimates workload- and space-specific analytical-to-reference disagreement, which parameterizes reference-consistent performance intervals and induces a Decision Domain for selective designated-reference evaluation. Under a valid domain-wide disagreement bound, this domain contains the designated-reference optimum; the sampling-based implementation is evaluated empirically on exhaustive-reference design spaces. Across ten reference-verifiable design spaces and seven LLM-training workloads, Fovea with 10% paired calibration recovers the exhaustive designated-reference optimum in all 70 evaluated pairs while achieving 4.13x average and 7.80x maximum end-to-end speedup.

</details>


### [246] [Beyond Peak TOPS/W: A System-Level Perspective on Hybrid Digital, Analogue and Neuromorphic Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03514)
*Eiman Kanjo,Varuna De Silva*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文论证混合数字-模拟计算是构建高能效AI系统的可信路径：以数字基础设施为基础，按需集成模拟/物理引擎，实现系统级能效优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI向云、端侧、物理系统广泛扩展，能耗与数据移动约束日益凸显，数字基础需要扩展以延续能效提升。

Method: 提出在数字编排下集成光子、存内、神经形态等模拟/物理引擎的混合架构；分析架构原则、负载适配、能耗核算、软件需求、局限与挑战。

Result: 模拟/物理引擎可在数字基础设施内承担特定加速角色，降低数据移动并加速矩阵与事件驱动负载；系统级度量应取代孤立TOPS/W作为评估标准。

Conclusion: 物理衬底仅在可量化系统级优势时获得扩展角色，数字编排管理集成、不确定性与回退；未来进展应以部署系统指标评估。

Abstract: The digital revolution, which progressively replaced analogue methods with digital circuits, has entered a new phase as AI expands across cloud infrastructure, mobile networks, wearables and physical systems, including drones and robots. Digital computing remains the general-purpose foundation of this expansion: it supports heterogeneous, on-device and decentralised AI through programmable control, mature software and decades of accumulated engineering infrastructure. Yet as energy and data-movement constraints become more significant, that same foundation is increasingly being extended rather than replaced by selected analogue and physical principles that it can host, configure and verify. Photonic, in-memory and neuromorphic architectures offer routes to reducing data movement and accelerating matrix-intensive and event-driven processing, not as alternatives to digital infrastructure but as specialised engines operating within it. This paper argues that hybrid digital--analogue computing represents a credible pathway towards more energy-efficient AI systems: one in which physical substrates earn an expanding role only where they deliver a measurable system-level advantage, under digital orchestration that manages integration, uncertainty and fallback. It examines the architectural principles, workload suitability, energy accounting, software requirements, limitations and open challenges associated with this transition, and argues that future progress should be evaluated through deployed-system metrics rather than isolated peak tera operations per second per watt (TOPS/W) claims.

</details>


### [247] [ComFuse: Fusing Complex Memory-Intensive Subgraphs with Compute-Intensive Kernels For Modern GPU Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03537)
*Di Mu,Tengyuan Jin,Zhenkun Wang,Jialin Yang,Yusen Li,Mian Huo,Shusong Guo,Gang Wang,Xiaoguang Liu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了ComFuse，一种自动化的GPU编译系统，通过新型算子融合策略为包含计算密集型和内存密集型子图的复杂计算图生成高性能内核，实验表明其性能优于TorchInductor。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习编译器将计算密集型算子和内存密集型子图分开优化，形成了固定的融合边界，限制了跨算子优化和片上数据重用。观察到下游内存密集型操作可以与计算密集型算子并发执行，从而将内存访问隐藏在计算过程中，但自动利用这一机会带来了新的编译挑战。

Method: 提出ComFuse系统，采用新型算子融合策略，为包含计算密集型算子和依赖丰富的内存密集型逐元素归约子图的复杂图结构生成高性能内核。支持back-to-back GEMM (B2BGEMM)模式的融合，扩展了对更复杂计算-内存交互模式的适用性。系统能够自动将高层张量子程序降低为优化的融合内核。

Result: ComFuse生成的融合内核在post-norm工作负载和各种复杂计算场景下，性能优于TorchInductor生成的内核，同时支持更灵活的融合模式。

Conclusion: ComFuse成功解决了异构计算图的优化问题，通过跨算子融合实现了计算与内存访问的有效重叠，证明了自动编译系统在处理复杂计算-内存交互模式方面的优越性，减少了对手动内核工程的需求。

Abstract: Modern deep learning workloads increasingly comprise heterogeneous computation graphs that combine compute-intensive operators with memory-intensive subgraphs. Existing deep learning compilers typically optimize these operator classes separately, creating rigid fusion boundaries that limit cross-operator optimization and on-chip data reuse. We observe that downstream memory-intensive operations can execute concurrently with compute-intensive operators, allowing their execution to be hidden behind computation; however, automatically exploiting this opportunity poses new compilation challenges.
  In this paper, we present ComFuse, an automated GPU compilation system that employs a novel operator fusion strategy to generate high-performance kernels for complex graph structures comprising compute-intensive operators and dependency-rich, memory-intensive elementwise-reduction subgraphs. ComFuse further supports the fusion of back-to-back GEMM (B2BGEMM) patterns, extending its applicability to more complex compute-memory interaction patterns. Additionally, it automatically lowers high-level tensor subprograms into optimized fused kernels, reducing the need for manual kernel engineering. Experimental results show that the fused kernels generated by ComFuse outperform those produced by TorchInductor across post-norm workloads and various complex computation scenarios, while supporting more flexible fusion patterns.

</details>


### [248] [Heterogeneous LLM Serving with General-Purpose Processing-Near-Memory for Retrieval-Based Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03555)
*Hyungkyu Ham,Junhyeong Bae,Seungheon Lee,Myeongjae Jeon,Gwangsun Kim*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了KARAT系统，一种面向检索式稀疏注意力的近存解码服务架构，通过将KV缓存移出GPU并配合调度优化，相比纯GPU基线实现2.09-6.13倍吞吐能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿大语言模型采用检索式稀疏注意力以服务百万级token上下文，然而GPU显存容量有限，难以承载大规模KV缓存。现有PIM/PNM设计假设已不适用于此类操作，亟需新的架构方案。

Method: 提出异构解码服务系统：GPU节点承载模型权重并执行投影与MoE层，近存(PNM)节点承载KV缓存并执行索引键及相关操作。设计KARAT器件，结合大容量LPDDR与面向检索索引的通用计算能力。提出机会式细粒度微批调度(OFMS)和上下文长度感知微批重平衡(CMR)以减少流水线气泡。

Result: 在三种SOTA模型和真实智能体轨迹上，所提系统在满足服务级目标(SLO)前提下，相比纯GPU基线吞吐/TDP提升2.09-6.13倍；运行无需训练的稀疏注意力方法时提升1.36-3.21倍。

Conclusion: KARAT系统有效解决检索式稀疏注意力场景下KV缓存GPU显存瓶颈，通过异构近存设计与调度协同，显著提升大模型长上下文推理的吞吐能效。

Abstract: This paper presents a heterogeneous decode-phase serving system that relocates the KV cache out of GPU memory, motivated by the retrieval-based sparse attention that recent frontier LLMs adopt to serve million-token contexts. It partitions a decode step by operation type: GPU nodes hold the model weights and execute the projections and MoE layers, while processing-near-memory (PNM) nodes hold the KV cache and index keys and execute every operation that reads them. We first show that the assumptions behind prior PIM and PNM designs no longer hold for these operations, and derive four design requirements for such a node. From these requirements, we propose KARAT (KV-cache-resident Accelerator for Retrieval-based ATtention), a general-purpose PNM design that is the design point meeting all four. A KARAT device combines large LPDDR capacity with general-purpose compute sized for the retrieval indexer, serving an operational intensity beyond what PIM/PNM designs built for low-intensity GEMV target while accommodating diverse sparse attention algorithms that fixed-function units cannot support as they evolve. To reduce pipeline bubbles as the two device types alternate between micro-batches, we further propose opportunistic, fine-grained micro-batch scheduling (OFMS), which hides expert all-to-all behind the other micro-batch's GEMMs, and context-length-aware micro-batch rebalancing (CMR), which equalizes their token counts despite the variance in context length. Across three state-of-the-art models and real agentic traces, our proposed system improves throughput per TDP under a service-level objective by 2.09-6.13x over a GPU-only baseline and runs training-free sparse attention methods with 1.36-3.21x improvements.

</details>


### [249] [DiffPower: GPU-Accelerated Differentiable Switching Power Analysis and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03778)
*Isaac Jacobson,Zheng Zhao,Rashmi Mehrotra,Guanglei Zhou,Vineet Rashingkar,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了DiffPower，一个基于GPU加速的可微分功耗分析框架，通过自动微分实现快速功耗梯度计算，在最大设计上实现1002倍加速，并支持单元尺寸优化和功耗病毒生成等下游应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代物理设计中，精确且可扩展的开关功耗分析仍是一个关键瓶颈，往往需要在计算速度和建模精度之间进行权衡。现有的功耗分析方法难以同时满足大规模设计的快速分析和优化需求。

Method: DiffPower将设计网表转换为PDK无关的字节码表示，通过反向模式自动微分实现解析梯度计算，采用混合传播方法融合解析建模与并行仿真，实现GPU加速的可微分功耗分析。

Result: 在最大评估设计上实现比单线程CPU传播高1002倍的加速，GPU优势随设计规模增大而增强；在十个工业和基准设计上达到中位翻转率相关系数r=0.96；功耗梯度计算速度比CPU有限差分方法快904倍，具有近乎完美的排序一致性；梯度加权单元尺寸优化在工业设计上实现比局部功耗启发式方法高2.98倍的改进；功耗病毒生成实现高2.13倍的翻转加权功耗。

Conclusion: DiffPower成功解决了功耗分析的速度与精度权衡问题，通过可微分编程实现高效功耗梯度计算，为下游功耗优化应用提供了强大支持，显著优于现有方法，尤其在大规模设计上优势更加明显。

Abstract: Accurate and scalable switching power analysis remains a critical bottleneck in modern physical design, often forcing a trade-off between computational speed and modeling fidelity. We present DiffPower, a GPU-accelerated framework for differentiable power analysis and optimization. DiffPower translates design netlists into a PDK-agnostic bytecode representation, enabling analytical gradient computation via reverse-mode automatic differentiation, achieving up to a $1{,}002\times$ speedup over single-threaded CPU propagation on the largest evaluated design, with the GPU advantage growing with design scale. A hybrid propagation methodology fusing analytical modeling with parallel simulation achieves a median toggle-rate correlation of $r{=}0.96$ across ten industrial and benchmark designs. The resulting \emph{power gradients}, computed up to $904\times$ faster than CPU finite-difference methods with near-perfect rank agreement, enable two downstream applications: (1) gradient-weighted cell sizing, which achieves up to $2.98\times$ improvement over local-power heuristics on industrial designs, with even stronger advantages at the 117K-cell scale where competing methods plateau; and (2) power virus generation via gradient ascent, which yields up to $2.13\times$ higher transition-weighted power, replacing a search process that traditionally requires hours.

</details>


### [250] [Heterogeneity-Aware Microscaling for Efficient Low-Bit LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03867)
*Junyi Luo,Xinting Jiang,Tai-Hao Wen,Ruichen Qi,Minxing Chu,Hongyi Wu,Gregory Kielian,Ben Laurie,Qirui Zhang,Quan Cheng,Dennis Sylvester,Mehdi Saligane*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出AdaMX（自适应微缩放）格式和加速器，通过块级和操作数级的异构性感知量化，在不增加等效位宽的情况下大幅恢复低比特LLM推理精度，仅需约1%额外系统能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MX格式（如MXFP4）在低比特LLM推理中精度损失严重，原因是其固定了块间元素格式或精度恢复方案，无法捕捉量化异构性。量化异构性存在于两个层次：1）不同块间首选的元素格式和精度恢复方案不同；2）权重和激活值等不同操作数需要不同的编码方式。

Method: 提出AdaMX格式和加速器，实现块级精度恢复方案选择和操作数级表示选择，不增加等效位宽（EBW）。设计覆盖两种块大小：高精度操作点和低EBW操作点。在22nm FD-SOI工艺上实现AI加速器原型，集成解码器、计算单元和量化逻辑。

Result: 相比仅使用FP4乘法器的MXFP4加速器，AdaMX仅增加约1%系统能耗。在低EBW操作点下，AdaMX比基线更准确且降低内存占用和能耗。在3B到70B的LLM上，AdaMX消除了MXFP4在常识推理上83%的精度损失，在MMLU上消除82%，同时消除NVFP4损失的43%和27%。在Gemma-4 12B上，四个视觉-语言基准测试均优于MXFP4，保持高达96%的FP16精度。

Conclusion: AdaMX成功解决了低比特LLM推理中的量化异构性问题，通过自适应格式选择在几乎不增加能耗的情况下大幅恢复精度，适用于从语言模型到多模态模型的广泛场景，为低比特推理提供了一种高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Microscaling (MX) is now the standard for low-bit large language model (LLM) inference. Its 4-bit form MXFP4 still loses substantial accuracy, because existing MX formats fix either the element format or the precision-recovery scheme across blocks, and thus capture only limited quantization heterogeneity. Quantization heterogeneity appears at two levels: 1) across blocks, the preferred element format and precision-recovery scheme vary; 2) across operands, weights and activations require different encoding. We introduce AdaMX (Adaptive Microscaling), a heterogeneity-aware format and accelerator. It selects the precision-recovery scheme per block and the representation per operand, at no increase in equivalent bit width (EBW). One design covers two block sizes, giving a higher-accuracy operating point and a lower-EBW operating point that saves storage. We implement a 22nm FD-SOI AI accelerator prototype with the proposed decoder, computing unit, and quantization logic. Against an otherwise identical MXFP4 accelerator with FP4-only multipliers, AdaMX adds about 1% system energy. At the lower-EBW point, AdaMX stays more accurate than the baseline while lowering both memory footprint and energy. Across LLMs from 3B to 70B, AdaMX removes 83% of the MXFP4 accuracy loss on commonsense and 82% on MMLU, and 43% and 27% of the NVFP4 loss. AdaMX also generalizes to multimodal models. On Gemma-4 12B, it leads MXFP4 on all four vision-language benchmarks and keeps up to 96% of FP16 accuracy.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [251] [Everyone Conforms, No One Believes: Pluralistic Ignorance in LLM Agent Populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02758)
*Yashwanth YS*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文发现基于LLM的多智能体系统能够稳健地复现“多元无知”现象：智能体在私下反对规范的情况下，公开一致性服从率高达64-94%。单一的“规范创业者”难以打破这种虚假共识，模型选择是影响模拟结果的关键自由度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统被广泛用于社会动力学模拟，但尚不清楚其能否捕捉“多元无知”这一关键社会心理现象。多元无知指多数人私下拒绝某规范却公开顺从，且认为只有自己持异见，该现象对规范持久性、社会运动和政治变革具有重要影响。

Method: 研究构建了一个包含10个领域、5个权威层级的100个场景的基准，基于人类多元无知文献，并评估来自6个组织的8个模型。研究者测试了智能体的公开顺从率，并引入单一“规范创业者”公开异议以测试打破虚假共识的可能性。通过提示组件消融实验，区分了顺从行为是涌现性还是指令驱动的。

Result: 智能体在私下反对规范的情况下，公开顺从率在64%至94%之间，且对领域和模型高度敏感。工作场所和社会关系场景几乎产生普遍顺从。对于7/8的模型，异议引发规范变革的成功率低于26%，其中某模型在所有场景中均为零；GPT-4o例外，成功率为48%。消融实验显示，去除虚假共识框架和顺从目标后，顺从率虽有下降但依然存在（52-92%），表明顺从是涌现性行为。

Conclusion: 模型选择是影响社会模拟结果的关键因素。由于异议几乎无法引发规范变革，LLM模拟可能系统性地高估社会规范的稳定性，忽略了驱动现实社会中规范变迁的脆弱临界点动力学。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems are increasingly used to simulate social dynamics, from opinion formation to collective decision-making. These simulations can reproduce certain social phenomena, but it is unknown whether they capture pluralistic ignorance, a state where a majority privately rejects a norm yet publicly conforms, each believing they are alone in dissenting. This phenomenon drives norm persistence, social movements, and political revolutions. We show that pluralistic ignorance emerges robustly in LLM agent populations. We construct a benchmark of 100 scenarios across 10 domains and 5 authority levels, grounded in the human pluralistic ignorance literature, and evaluate 8 models from 6 organizations. Agents publicly conform at rates of 64 to 94% despite privately opposing the norm. Conformity is domain-sensitive (workplace and social relationship scenarios produce near-universal compliance) and highly model-dependent, though uncorrelated with capability. We test whether a single "norm entrepreneur" can break the false consensus by publicly dissenting. For 7 of 8 models, cascades succeed less than 26% of the time, with one model showing zero cascades across all scenarios. GPT-4o is a notable outlier at 48%, revealing qualitatively distinct dynamics across model families. A prompt component ablation across all 8 models establishes that conformity is emergent rather than instruction-driven: removing both the false-consensus framing and fit-in goal reduces conformity but does not eliminate it (52 to 92% in the minimal condition). Our findings identify model selection as an unacknowledged degree of freedom that fundamentally shapes simulation outcomes. More broadly, the near-absence of cascades suggests LLM simulations may systematically overestimate the stability of social norms, missing the fragile tipping-point dynamics that drive real-world norm change in human societies.

</details>


### [252] [Stateful Governance for Concurrent Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02764)
*Yuxiang Peng,Xiaodi Wu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究了并发智能体系统中的状态化治理问题，识别了“过时授权”这一核心故障模式，提出了“策略状态可串行化”正确性条件，并设计了Provenact运行时架构来防止策略状态变更导致的授权失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体正从建议型接口转向执行关键操作的系统（如退款、库存预订、云资源配置、金融转账等），现有治理机制通常在请求时基于可用信息判断操作是否允许。然而，对于有状态的策略，请求时的视图可能不完整——预算、库存、审批状态和风险信号可能在效果发生前发生变化，导致先前的授权或许可变得过时。

Method: 提出“策略状态可串行化”（policy-state serializability）正确性条件，要求已提交的效果必须能够解释为在效果发生前一刻的策略状态下被授权。设计并实现Provenact运行时架构，将策略保持为可审查的程序，同时协调状态和效果以保持决策的有效性。采用PostgreSQL后端构建原型系统进行实验验证。

Result: 在PostgreSQL支持的Provenact原型实验中：系统能够防止将策略状态作为普通请求上下文传递的基线方法所遗漏的过时授权；在无关工作继续进行时保持延迟审批；将策略演进主要保持在策略文本而非可信提供者代码中；在脚本化、无LLM的采购工作流中避免了策略违规，而智能体治理基线在共享预算和库存上产生过时授权。

Conclusion: Provenact为在智能体框架和提供者支持的领域中集成状态化治理边界提供了一条路径，特别适用于智能体对共享资源执行操作的场景，为并发智能体系统的安全治理提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: AI agents are moving from advisory interfaces into systems that execute consequential operations: issuing refunds, reserving scarce inventory, provisioning cloud resources, and initiating financial transfers. These workflows require governance over effects, not only over model outputs. Existing safeguards often decide whether an action is allowed from the information available when the action is requested. For stateful policies, that request-time view may be incomplete: budgets, inventory, approval status, and risk signals can change before the effect occurs, making an earlier authorization or approval stale.
  This paper studies stateful governance for concurrent agentic systems. We identify stale authorization as the core failure mode and define policy-state serializability, a correctness condition requiring committed effects to be explainable as authorized against the policy state immediately before they occur. We present Provenact, a runtime architecture that keeps policies as reviewable programs while coordinating the state and effects needed to preserve their decisions. In experiments with a PostgreSQL-backed prototype of Provenact, the system prevents stale authorizations missed by baselines that pass policy state as ordinary request context, preserves delayed approvals while unrelated work proceeds, keeps policy evolution mostly in policy text rather than trusted provider code, and avoids policy violations in a scripted, LLM-free procurement workflow where agent-governance baselines produce stale authorizations over shared budgets and inventory. More broadly, Provenact suggests a path for integrating stateful governance boundaries into agent frameworks and provider-backed domains where agents act on shared resources.

</details>


### [253] [Emergence of Biased Consensus in Multi-Agent LLM Debates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02827)
*Maya Okawa*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该论文研究了多智能体LLM辩论中的集体偏见问题，发现噪声会驱动偏见的涌现，并提出了物理学启发的理论框架来解释这一现象，发现存在向集体偏见的相变，而智能体异质性可以抑制这一过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM辩论在决策和问题解决任务中表现优异，但其安全性和公平性风险尚不明确。交互可能会放大单个LLM的偏见，引发对实际部署的担忧。因此，需要深入理解多智能体系统中集体偏见涌现的机制。

Method: 提出基于物理学启发的社会动力学理论模型的分析框架。通过控制实验验证理论预测，研究从众行为、初始偏见和辩论噪声对集体偏见涌现的影响。研究智能体异质性对相变的抑制作用，并在实际决策任务（投资决策、LLM-as-a-judge评估）中验证发现的普适性。

Result: 发现噪声（如LLM采样温度）是集体偏见涌现的关键驱动因素。预测当从众行为超过临界阈值时，会发生向集体偏见的相变。实验观察到有限尺寸交叉现象，证实存在潜在的相变。智能体异质性能够平滑（钝化）这一转变，从而抑制偏见的涌现。这些发现在现实决策任务中具有普适性。

Conclusion: 该研究为多智能体LLM系统的安全性和公平性提供了理论框架和实证依据，揭示了集体偏见涌现的物理机制，为设计更安全的多智能体AI系统提供了指导，特别是在控制偏见传播和增强智能体多样性方面。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM debates achieve strong performance on decision-making tasks as well as problem-solving benchmarks, yet their safety and fairness risks remain poorly understood. Notably, interaction can amplify the biases of single LLMs, raising concerns for real-world deployment. We identify the emergence of collective (often biased) norms in multi-agent LLM debates and show that noise (e.g., LLM sampling temperature) is a key driver. To explain this, we propose an analytical framework drawing on physics-inspired theoretical models of social dynamics. We predict a phase transition to collective bias when conformity surpasses a critical threshold given the LLMs' initial bias and debate noise. We test the theoretical predictions through controlled experiments and observe a finite-size crossover consistent with an underlying phase transition. We further find that agent heterogeneity suppresses emergence by smoothing (rounding) this transition. Finally, we show that these insights generalize to realistic decision-making tasks, including investment decisions and LLM-as-a-judge evaluation.

</details>


### [254] [SABRE: A Multi-Agent Approach for Selecting Out-of-Distribution Detectors Under a Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02959)
*Mary Wisell,Salimeh Sekeh*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 论文揭示了现有OOD检测器在跨域部署时性能不稳定甚至反转的问题，提出SABRE框架，通过三个语言模型代理在推理时动态选择和校准检测器，实现可靠的分布外检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言模型后验分布外(OOD)检测方法假设在基准测试上表现良好的检测器在实际部署时依然可靠。然而本文发现这一假设在跨域场景下失效：在单一固定编码器上，某个域上表现最佳的检测器在另一个域上可能发生性能反转（将域内输入判断为比真实异常值更异常），且最佳检测器随域变化而变化，因此不存在一个固定的检测器选择能在所有域上都保持可靠。

Method: 提出SABRE（选择性代理预算可靠性集成）框架，包含三个语言模型代理和一个检测器库：1）Selector（选择器）：决定下一步咨询哪个检测器；2）Reporter（报告器）：整合每个输入的证据信息；3）Analyst（分析器）：在部署域的小规模标注样本上校准检测器可靠性，用于加权选择和聚合过程。系统在有限的查询预算下进行按域选择，推断当前操作域并跟踪最强检测器。此外，论文还提出了四种多模态密度检测器加入检测器库。

Result: SABRE能够在没有先验知识的情况下追踪每个域上最强的检测器，在传统检测器发生性能反转的场景下恢复可靠检测，在检测器表现正常的场景下收敛至该检测器。组件分析表明三个代理相互补充：Reporter的反馈带来持续增益，Analyst的校准对抵抗性能反转起决定性作用，能够排除不可靠检测器从而避免聚合过程抵消可靠检测器的效果。

Conclusion: 由于不存在固定规则能够在跨域场景下保持可靠，可靠性必须在部署阶段而非基准测试阶段建立。SABRE展示了可以通过自动化方式在部署时建立可靠性，为视觉-语言模型的OOD检测提供了一种可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Post-hoc out-of-distribution (OOD) detection for vision-language models assumes that a detector chosen on a benchmark stays reliable once deployed. We show this fails across domains: on a single frozen encoder, a detector that leads in one domain can invert in another, scoring in-distribution inputs as more anomalous than genuine outliers, and the best detector changes from domain to domain, so no fixed choice is reliable throughout. We introduce SABRE (Selective Agentic Budgeted Reliability Ensemble,) which replaces this fixed choice with per-regime selection at inference. Three language-model agents reason over a library of post-hoc detectors under a bounded query budget: a Selector chooses which detector to consult next, a Reporter consolidates the evidence for each input, and an Analyst calibrates detector reliability on a small labeled sample held out from the deployment domain and disjoint from the test data, weighting selection and aggregation without ever observing a scored input's label. The library includes four multimodal density detectors we propose. Inferring the operating regime from data, SABRE tracks the strongest detector in each domain without prior knowledge of it, recovering reliable detection where a conventional detector inverts and converging to that detector where it is sound. A component analysis shows the agents are complementary: the Reporter's feedback yields consistent gains, and the Analyst's calibration is decisive against inversion, ruling out unreliable detectors so that aggregation no longer cancels the sound ones. Since no fixed rule can be trusted across domains, reliability must be established at deployment rather than assumed from a benchmark, and SABRE shows this can be done automatically.

</details>


### [255] [When Truth Is Distributed: Misinformation Derails Collective Fact Recovery in LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03421)
*Chenfei Yan,Zeyang Yue,Feifei Zhao,Erliang Lin,Lu Jia,Haibo Tong,Mingyang Lyu,Chengyi Sun,Yi Zeng*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究发现LLM多智能体协作系统在面对欺骗性信息时极为脆弱，真实信息恢复率从72.50%骤降至14.17%，且虚假信息比真实信息更容易被采纳和传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法过于关注最终结果，而忽视了分布式信息聚合的可靠性和传播动力学，特别是通信过程中局部错误如何演变为集体风险的问题。

Method: 提出Hi-Agreement框架，通过配对实验（全诚实协作 vs 关键证据持有者的受控欺骗），结合多阶段投票、证词采纳和证据根谱系传播分析，在120个五智能体物体移动环境中评估3个同质LLM多智能体系统。

Result: 真实信息恢复率从72.50%下降至14.17%；虚假证词比真实证词更易被采纳，传播至更高阶，并在欺骗者退出后仍通过诚实智能体持续传播；无一手证据的观察者虽能抑制错误共识，但无法提升真相恢复。

Conclusion: 分布式事实恢复具有脆弱性，其内在机制是：虚假证据一旦进入通信系统，便会通过其他智能体的采纳和持续传播获得集体影响力，揭示了LLM多智能体系统在信息可靠性方面的重大隐患。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems promise effective collaborative reasoning, but communication may amplify local errors into collective risks. Existing evaluations emphasize final outcomes, leaving the reliability and propagation dynamics of distributed information aggregation unclear. We introduce Hi-Agreement, a controlled evaluation framework that strictly pairs all-honest collaboration with controlled deception by a key evidence holder and analyzes the aggregation process through multi-stage voting, testimony adoption, and evidence-root lineage propagation. Using 120 five-agent object-movement environments where partial observations jointly determine a unique endpoint, we evaluate 3 homogeneous LLM-based multi-agent systems. Across these paired conditions, aggregate truth recovery falls from 72.50% to 14.17%, with significant declines for every system. Process tracing and exit ablations show that a single false testimony is adopted more readily than truthful testimony, propagates to higher orders, and persists through honest agents after the deceiver exits. Observers without first-hand evidence suppress incorrect consensus but do not improve truth recovery. Together, these findings reveal both the fragility of distributed fact recovery and its underlying mechanism: false evidence gains collective influence through its adoption and continued propagation by other agents after entering communication.

</details>


### [256] [Group Perspective Matters: Regulating Debate Relationships Can Mitigate Blind Conformity in Multi-Agent Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03648)
*Hao Wu,Shoucheng Song,Chang Yao,Haoyu Wang,Huaiyu Wan,Youfang Lin,Kai Lv*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出DEAR框架，通过群体视角动态调节辩论关系，利用多智能体强化学习缓解多智能体辩论中LLM的盲目从众问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体辩论（MAD）中的大语言模型容易产生盲目从众现象，现有基于置信度或困惑度的个体评估方法无法反映推理正确性，甚至会加剧从众问题。

Method: 提出DEAR框架，从群体交互视角出发：1）量化共识与分歧作为群体证据；2）感知群体咨询倾向和不确定性；3）引入选择RL智能体动态选择参考同伴；4）采用行为RL智能体自适应调整生成行为；通过多智能体强化学习联合优化。

Result: DEAR在多项实验中表现出优越性能，同时显著降低了token消耗。

Conclusion: 通过动态调节辩论关系，DEAR有效缓解了MAD中的盲目从众问题，验证了从群体视角优化多智能体辩论的可行性。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Debate (MAD) improves the reasoning performance of Large Language Models (LLMs) through multi-round interaction. However, LLMs in MAD are highly susceptible to blind conformity. Existing individual evaluation methods, typically based on confidence or perplexity, fail to reflect the correctness of reasoning and may even exacerbate blind conformity. To address this, we shift the perspective from individual evaluation to group interaction. We define mutual referencing among LLMs as \textbf{Debate Relationships} and recognize that regulating these relationships is the key to mitigating blind conformity. In this paper, we propose a novel framework for \textbf{D}ynamically r\textbf{E}gulating deb\textbf{A}te \textbf{R}elationships (DEAR) from the group perspective. At first, DEAR quantifies consensus and divergence as \textit{group evidence} to capture the debate state. Then, DEAR operates through three stages: 1) What: perceiving group consultation tendency and uncertainty; 2) Who: introducing a Selection RL-Agent to dynamically select reference peers; and 3) How: adopting a Behavior RL-Agent to adaptively adjust generation behaviors. Notably, we formulate the execution of the two RL-Agents as a sequential decision-making process, jointly optimizing via multi-agent reinforcement learning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DEAR achieves superior performance while significantly reducing token consumption.

</details>


### [257] [An Actionable Diagnosis of Multilingual, Multi-Agent Planning Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03735)
*Vikas Pahuja,Jonathan Brokman,Omer Hofman,Tamir Nizri,Daniel Vishna,Seraphina Goldfarb-Tarrant,Kelly Marchisio,Hisashi Kojima,Roman Vainshtein*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析多智能体系统中规划器的失败案例，提出了规划-基础失败的可操作分类法，并基于此开发了TART方法，在多语言场景下显著提升了系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言多智能体系统在非英语任务上表现严重退化，但现有研究未能深入分析用户请求转换为可执行计划时关键信息如何丢失。研究旨在识别和分类这些信息丢失的具体机制，特别是针对低资源语言的场景。

Method: 研究将规划器作为请求到动作的接口，从真实世界任务执行失败中提取出规划-基础失败的可操作分类法。基于此分类法，提出了TART（Taxonomy-Guided Actionable Representation）方法，该方法将分类法的关键方面显式呈现给规划器和下游子智能体，使系统更好地理解和处理跨语言任务。

Result: LLM分析显示，随着语言资源可用性降低，规划-基础失败在失败执行中的占比增加，在低资源语言中效果最明显。TART方法在多语言、三个LLM骨干网络、两个数据集和两种智能体配置下均表现出一致的性能提升。在多语言GAIA基准测试上，将现有最优系统在11种语言（涵盖低资源到高资源设置）上的平均准确率提升了5.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过系统性分析和分类规划器失败模式，并基于分类法设计显式表示方法，可以有效缓解多语言多智能体系统中的性能退化问题，为跨语言AI系统设计提供了新的改进方向。

Abstract: Multilingual multi-agent systems exhibit substantial degradation beyond English, yet prior work rarely identifies how task-critical information is lost when user requests are converted into executable plans. We study the planner in a multi-agent system as the request-to-action interface and derive an actionable taxonomy of planning-grounding failures from failed real-world task executions. LLM-based analysis shows that these failures constitute an increasing share of unsuccessful executions as language-resource availability declines, with the strongest effects in low-resource languages. To test whether the taxonomy supports mitigation, we introduce TART, Taxonomy-Guided Actionable Representation, that makes the taxonomy's key aspects explicit to the planner and downstream sub-agents. Across multiple languages, three LLM backbones, two datasets, and two agentic configurations, TART consistently improves performance. On multilingual GAIA, it raises a state-of-the-art system's accuracy by 5.6 percentage points averaged across eleven languages spanning low- to high-resource settings.

</details>


### [258] [History Matters: Meta-policy Delegation with Heterogeneous Multi-agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03833)
*Ziqing Lu,Avinash Reddy Mudireddy,Sarra Alqahtani,Weiyu Xu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文针对异构多智能体系统中的任务委托问题，提出了基于MARL的委托训练方法、历史依赖的策略框架以及多维货币机制，以实现资源受限场景下的高效协作与成本最小化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来决策系统中AI智能体的重要性日益增加，异构多智能体系统（MAS）成员具有不同的能力和运行成本，需要在资源受限场景下实现任务的有效完成，因此研究智能体之间的任务委托机制成为关键问题。

Method: 提出基于多智能体强化学习（MARL）的委托训练方法，使智能体能够做出序列委托决策并最小化总执行成本；扩展到具有规定委托拓扑的MARL；引入两个新框架：历史依赖策略框架（策略不仅依赖当前状态还依赖交互历史）和多维货币机制框架以促进MAS的协作与委托。

Result: 历史依赖策略框架即使在完全可观测环境中也能改善协调效果，突破了传统MARL方法仅依赖当前状态的限制；货币机制框架为多智能体系统的协作与委托提供了新的激励手段。

Conclusion: 本研究为异构多智能体系统中的任务委托与协作提供了系统性的解决方案，所提出的方法和框架能够有效降低执行成本并提升协调效率，为未来决策系统中AI智能体的应用奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: AI agents are expected to play an increasingly important role in future decision-making systems. In this paper, we consider collaborative systems composed of heterogeneous multi-agent systems (MAS), where their members have different capabilities and operating costs. We study how agents can delegate tasks to one another so that certain research tasks can be completed effectively under resource-constrained scenarios. We first develop a multi-agent reinforcement learning-based (MARL) delegation training that enables agents to make sequential delegation decisions while minimizing the total execution cost. We then extend this approach to MARL with prescribed delegation topologies. Furthermore, we introduce two new frameworks for collaboration and delegation in multi-agent systems. The first framework proposes that an agent's policy depends not only on the current state of the underlying Markov decision process but also on the interaction history, including previous joint actions. This history-dependent formulation can improve coordination even in fully observable environments, where conventional MARL methods typically restrict policies to depend only on the current state. The second framework proposes a novel, potentially multi-dimensional monetary mechanism to facilitate the collaboration and delegation for MAS.

</details>
